##### ladderize()

```ts
ladderize(ascending): void;
```

Defined in: [models/PhyloTree.ts:125](https://github.com/pentamorfico/hoodini-viz/blob/f80424909b837fad609fbf45042999d210c04024/src/models/PhyloTree.ts#L125)

Ladderize the tree by sorting branches at each node by the number of descendant leaves.
This produces a cleaner visual representation matching Taxonium's algorithm exactly.
Uses iterative traversal to avoid stack overflow on large trees.

###### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Default value | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `ascending` | `boolean` | `true` | If true, smaller clades go first (top); if false, larger clades go first |

###### Returns

`void`

### HoodiniVizProps

Defined in: [components/HoodiniViz.tsx:145](https://github.com/pentamorfico/hoodini-viz/blob/f80424909b837fad609fbf45042999d210c04024/src/components/HoodiniViz.tsx#L145)

Props for the HoodiniViz component.

HoodiniViz is the core visualization component that renders phylogenetic trees,
genomic neighborhoods, protein domains, and homology links using deck.gl.

| <a id="domainsbygene"></a> `domainsByGene?` | `Record`\<`string`, `object`[]\> | Domain annotations organized by gene ID. **Example** `{ "gene1": [{ domainId: "PF00001", start: 10, end: 100 }] }` | [components/HoodiniViz.tsx:178](https://github.com/pentamorfico/hoodini-viz/blob/f80424909b837fad609fbf45042999d210c04024/src/components/HoodiniViz.tsx#L178) |

### HoodiniDashboard

```ts
const HoodiniDashboard: ForwardRefExoticComponent<HoodiniDashboardProps & RefAttributes<HoodiniDashboardRef>>;
```

Defined in: [HoodiniDashboard.tsx:1741](https://github.com/pentamorfico/hoodini-viz/blob/f80424909b837fad609fbf45042999d210c04024/src/HoodiniDashboard.tsx#L1741)

HoodiniDashboard - Complete genomic neighborhood visualization dashboard.

Provides:
- Automatic data loading (Parquet/TSV)
- Sidebar with all controls
- HoodiniViz visualization
- DataGrid for tabular views
- Full theme support
- Imperative API via ref

***
